1. Glucose-6-phosphate → 6-phosphogluconolactone (by G6PD)
2. 6-phosphogluconolactone → 6-phosphogluconate (by PGL)

The Entner-Doudoroff pathway:
1. Glucose → glucose-6-phosphate (by hexokinase)
2. Glucose-6-phosphate → 6-phosphogluconolactone (by G6PD)
3. 6-phosphogluconolactone → 6-phosphogluconate (by PGL)
4. 6-phosphogluconate → KDPG + H2O (by Edd, 6-phosphogluconate dehydratase)
5. KDPG → pyruvate + G3P (by Eda, KDPG aldolase)

So the ED pathway doesn&#x27;t use 6-PGDH. The steps 1-3 are shared with the pentose phosphate pathway.
